+<!-- Mediator Debug Dashboard — Tree Explorer, Node Detail, Actions -->
+<!-- Phase 7C-E: full JS for the mediator debug view -->
+<script>
+    // ── Mediator state ────────────────────────────────────────────
+    let _mediatorDiagData = null;
+    let _mediatorSelectedPath = null;
+    let _mediatorSelectedBranch = null;  // which branch header is active
+    let _mediatorAgeTimer = null;
+    let _mediatorCollapsed = {};       // branch → bool (persisted in sessionStorage)
+    let _mediatorEventsListener = null;
+    let _mediatorEvents = [];
+    let _mediatorEventsPaused = false;
+
+    // Restore collapsed state from session
+    try {
+        const saved = sessionStorage.getItem('_mediatorCollapsed');
+        if (saved) _mediatorCollapsed = JSON.parse(saved);
+    } catch(e) {}
+
+    // ── Load & Render ─────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+    async function loadMediatorDiag() {
+        const summary = document.getElementById('mediator-summary');
+        const tree = document.getElementById('mediator-tree');
+        try {
+            summary.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner"></span>&nbsp; Loading mediator diagnostics…';
+            const data = await api('/mediator/diag');
+            _mediatorDiagData = data;
+            _debugData.mediator = data;
+            _mediatorRenderSummary(data);
+            _mediatorRenderTree(data);
+            _mediatorRenderGraph(data);
+            _mediatorRenderEventStream();
+            _mediatorStartAgeTimer();
+            // Show root overview on first load (preserve selection on refresh)
+            if (!_mediatorSelectedPath && !_mediatorSelectedBranch) {
+                _mediatorSelectRoot();
+            }
+        } catch(e) {
+            summary.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--danger)">⚠ ${esc(e.message)}</span>`;
+            if (tree) tree.innerHTML = '';
+        }
+    }
+
+    // ── Summary bar ───────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+    function _mediatorRenderSummary(data) {
+        const el = document.getElementById('mediator-summary');
+        if (!el || !data) return;
+
+        const tree = data.tree || {};
+        const refreshCount = (data.refreshing || []).length;
+
+        const parts = [];
+        parts.push(`<span style="font-weight:600">seq: ${data.seq || 0}</span>`);
+        parts.push(`<span>${tree.registered || 0} nodes</span>`);
+        parts.push(`<span style="color:var(--success)">${data.cached || 0} cached</span>`);
+
+        if (data.stale > 0) {
+            parts.push(`<span style="background:rgba(251,191,36,0.15);color:#d97706;padding:1px 8px;border-radius:10px;font-weight:500">${data.stale} stale</span>`);
+        } else {
+            parts.push(`<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">0 stale</span>`);
+        }
+
+        if (refreshCount > 0) {
+            parts.push(`<span style="background:rgba(99,102,241,0.12);color:#6366f1;padding:1px 8px;border-radius:10px;font-weight:500;animation:pulse 1.5s ease-in-out infinite">${refreshCount} refreshing</span>`);
+        }
+
+        parts.push(`<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">${data.subscriptions || 0} subs</span>`);
+
+        const execIcon = data.has_executor ? '✓' : '✗';
+        const execColor = data.has_executor ? 'var(--success)' : 'var(--text-muted)';
+        parts.push(`<span style="color:${execColor}">executor: ${execIcon}</span>`);
+
+        el.innerHTML = parts.join('<span style="color:var(--border-subtle);margin:0 4px">│</span>');
+    }
+
+    // ── Tree Explorer ─────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+    function _mediatorRenderTree(data) {
+        const container = document.getElementById('mediator-tree');
+        if (!container || !data || !data.entries) {
+            if (container) container.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.74rem;padding:12px">No entries</div>';
+            return;
+        }
+
+        // Group entries by first path segment
+        const branches = {};
+        const paths = Object.keys(data.entries).sort();
+        for (const p of paths) {
+            const seg = p.split('.')[0];
+            if (!branches[seg]) branches[seg] = [];
+            branches[seg].push(p);
+        }
+
+        let html = `<div onclick="_mediatorSelectRoot()" style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:6px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-subtle);margin-bottom:4px;cursor:pointer;background:${!_mediatorSelectedPath && !_mediatorSelectedBranch ? 'rgba(99,102,241,0.08)' : 'transparent'};border-radius:var(--radius-sm) var(--radius-sm) 0 0" onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(99,102,241,0.05)'" onmouseout="this.style.background='${!_mediatorSelectedPath && !_mediatorSelectedBranch ? 'rgba(99,102,241,0.08)' : 'transparent'}'">
+            <span style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.76rem;color:var(--text-secondary)">🌳 root</span>
+            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-ghost" onclick="event.stopPropagation();loadMediatorDiag()" style="font-size:0.65rem;padding:2px 6px" title="Reload diagnostics">↻</button>
+        </div>`;
+
+        const branchNames = Object.keys(branches).sort();
+        for (const branch of branchNames) {
+            const nodes = branches[branch];
+            const collapsed = _mediatorCollapsed[branch] === true;
+            const arrow = collapsed ? '▶' : '▼';
+
+            // Count stats for this branch
+            let bCached = 0, bStale = 0, bCold = 0, bRefreshing = 0;
+            for (const p of nodes) {
+                const e = data.entries[p];
+                const isRefr = (data.refreshing || []).includes(p);
+                if (isRefr) bRefreshing++;
+                else if (e && e.stale) bStale++;
+                else if (e && e.cached !== false && e.age_s !== undefined) bCached++;
+                else bCold++;
+            }
+            const bSummary = [];
+            if (bCached > 0) bSummary.push(`<span style="color:var(--success)">${bCached}✓</span>`);
+            if (bStale > 0) bSummary.push(`<span style="color:#d97706">${bStale}⚠</span>`);
+            if (bCold > 0) bSummary.push(`<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">${bCold}∅</span>`);
+            if (bRefreshing > 0) bSummary.push(`<span style="color:#6366f1">${bRefreshing}🔄</span>`);
+
+            const isBranchSelected = _mediatorSelectedBranch === branch;
+            const branchBg = isBranchSelected ? 'rgba(99,102,241,0.08)' : 'transparent';
+
+            html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:5px 8px;font-size:0.74rem;user-select:none;background:${branchBg};border-left:${isBranchSelected ? '2px solid var(--accent)' : '2px solid transparent'}">
+                <span onclick="_mediatorToggleBranch('${esc(branch)}')" style="cursor:pointer;font-size:0.65rem;width:10px">${arrow}</span>
+                <span onclick="_mediatorSelectBranch('${esc(branch)}')" style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;color:${isBranchSelected ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--text-secondary)'}">${esc(branch)}</span>
+                <span style="font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text-muted);font-weight:400">(${nodes.length})</span>
+                <span style="font-size:0.62rem;display:flex;gap:4px;margin-left:auto">${bSummary.join(' ')}</span>
+            </div>`;
+
+            if (!collapsed) {
+                for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
+                    const isLast = (i === nodes.length - 1);
+                    html += _mediatorTreeRow(nodes[i], data.entries[nodes[i]], data.refreshing || [], isLast);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // Legend
+        html += `<div style="padding:8px 8px 4px;margin-top:8px;border-top:1px solid var(--border-subtle);font-size:0.62rem;color:var(--text-muted);line-height:1.6">
+            <span style="color:var(--success)">●</span> cached&nbsp;&nbsp;
+            <span style="color:var(--text-muted)">○</span> cold&nbsp;&nbsp;
+            <span style="color:var(--success)">✓</span> fresh&nbsp;&nbsp;
+            <span style="color:#d97706">⚠</span> stale&nbsp;&nbsp;
+            <span>💤</span> dormant&nbsp;&nbsp;
+            <span>🔄</span> refreshing&nbsp;&nbsp;
+            <span style="color:var(--text-muted)">∅</span> never&nbsp;&nbsp;
+            <span style="color:var(--text-muted)">∞</span> always
+        </div>`;
+
+        container.innerHTML = html;
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorTreeRow(path, entry, refreshingList, isLast) {
+        const isSelected = _mediatorSelectedPath === path;
+        const isRefreshing = refreshingList.includes(path);
+
+        // Tree connector
+        const connector = isLast ? '└─' : '├─';
+
+        // Cache indicator (● cached, ○ not cached)
+        let cacheIcon, cacheColor;
+        if (entry && entry.cached !== false && entry.age_s !== undefined) {
+            cacheIcon = '●'; cacheColor = 'var(--success)';
+        } else {
+            cacheIcon = '○'; cacheColor = 'var(--text-muted)';
+        }
+
+        // Status indicator (right side)
+        let statusIcon;
+        if (isRefreshing) {
+            statusIcon = '🔄';
+        } else if (entry && entry.stale) {
+            statusIcon = '<span style="color:#d97706">⚠</span>';
+        } else if (entry && entry.cached !== false && entry.age_s !== undefined) {
+            statusIcon = '<span style="color:var(--success)">✓</span>';
+        } else if (entry && entry.cached === false) {
+            statusIcon = '<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">∅</span>';
+        } else {
+            statusIcon = '<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">∅</span>';
+        }
+
+        // Short name (everything after first dot)
+        const shortName = path.split('.').slice(1).join('.') || path;
+
+        // Age display
+        let ageStr = '—';
+        if (entry && entry.age_s !== undefined) {
+            ageStr = _mediatorFormatAge(entry.age_s);
+        }
+
+        const bgColor = isSelected ? 'rgba(99,102,241,0.08)' : 'transparent';
+        const borderLeft = isSelected ? '2px solid var(--accent)' : '2px solid transparent';
+
+        return `<div onclick="_mediatorSelectNode('${esc(path)}')" data-mediator-path="${esc(path)}" data-computed-at="${entry && entry.age_s !== undefined ? (Date.now()/1000 - entry.age_s) : ''}" style="cursor:pointer;padding:2px 8px 2px 16px;font-size:0.72rem;font-family:var(--font-mono,'Consolas','Monaco',monospace);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0;border-left:${borderLeft};background:${bgColor};transition:background 0.15s;line-height:1.5" onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(99,102,241,0.05)'" onmouseout="this.style.background='${bgColor}'">
+            <span style="color:var(--text-muted);white-space:pre">${connector} </span>
+            <span style="color:${cacheColor}">${cacheIcon}</span>
+            <span style="flex:1;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--text-primary);padding:0 6px" title="${esc(path)}">${esc(shortName)}</span>
+            <span class="mediator-age" style="color:var(--text-muted);min-width:36px;text-align:right;padding-right:6px">${ageStr}</span>
+            <span style="min-width:16px;text-align:center">${statusIcon}</span>
+        </div>`;
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorFormatAge(seconds) {
+        if (seconds === undefined || seconds === null) return '—';
+        if (seconds < 1) return '<1s';
+        if (seconds < 60) return Math.round(seconds) + 's';
+        if (seconds < 3600) return Math.round(seconds / 60) + 'm';
+        return Math.round(seconds / 3600) + 'h';
+    }
+
+    // ── Branch collapse ───────────────────────────────────────────
+
+    function _mediatorToggleBranch(branch) {
+        _mediatorCollapsed[branch] = !_mediatorCollapsed[branch];
+        try { sessionStorage.setItem('_mediatorCollapsed', JSON.stringify(_mediatorCollapsed)); } catch(e) {}
+        if (_mediatorDiagData) _mediatorRenderTree(_mediatorDiagData);
+    }
+
+    // ── Root selection (global overview) ─────────────────────
+
+    function _mediatorSelectRoot() {
+        _mediatorSelectedPath = null;
+        _mediatorSelectedBranch = null;
+        if (_mediatorDiagData) _mediatorRenderTree(_mediatorDiagData);
+
+        const detail = document.getElementById('mediator-detail');
+        if (!detail || !_mediatorDiagData) return;
+
+        const data = _mediatorDiagData;
+        const entries = data.entries || {};
+        const allPaths = Object.keys(entries).sort();
+        const refreshing = data.refreshing || [];
+
+        // Count global stats
+        let cached = 0, stale = 0, cold = 0, refr = 0;
+        for (const p of allPaths) {
+            const e = entries[p];
+            if (refreshing.includes(p)) refr++;
+            else if (e && e.stale) stale++;
+            else if (e && e.cached !== false && e.age_s !== undefined) cached++;
+            else cold++;
+        }
+
+        // Group by branch for overview table
+        const branches = {};
+        for (const p of allPaths) {
+            const seg = p.split('.')[0];
+            if (!branches[seg]) branches[seg] = { total: 0, cached: 0, stale: 0, cold: 0 };
+            branches[seg].total++;
+            const e = entries[p];
+            if (refreshing.includes(p)) { /* skip */ }
+            else if (e && e.stale) branches[seg].stale++;
+            else if (e && e.cached !== false && e.age_s !== undefined) branches[seg].cached++;
+            else branches[seg].cold++;
+        }
+
+        let html = `<div style="padding:12px 16px">
+            <div style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--text-primary);margin-bottom:12px">🌳 Mediator Overview</div>
+
+            <div style="display:flex;gap:16px;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:var(--radius-sm);background:var(--bg-secondary);font-size:0.72rem;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">
+                <span><strong>${allPaths.length}</strong> total nodes</span>
+                <span style="color:var(--success)"><strong>${cached}</strong> fresh</span>
+                <span style="color:#d97706"><strong>${stale}</strong> stale</span>
+                <span style="color:var(--text-muted)"><strong>${cold}</strong> cold</span>`;
+        if (refr > 0) html += `<span style="color:#6366f1"><strong>${refr}</strong> refreshing</span>`;
+        html += `<span>seq <strong>#${data.seq || 0}</strong></span>
+                <span>executor: ${data.has_executor ? '<span style="color:var(--success)">✓</span>' : '<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">✗</span>'}</span>
+            </div>
+
+            <table style="width:100%;font-size:0.72rem;border-collapse:collapse">
+                <tr style="color:var(--text-muted);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-subtle)">
+                    <td style="padding:4px 8px 4px 0;font-weight:600">Branch</td>
+                    <td style="padding:4px 8px;text-align:center">Nodes</td>
+                    <td style="padding:4px 8px;text-align:center">✓</td>
+                    <td style="padding:4px 8px;text-align:center">⚠</td>
+                    <td style="padding:4px 8px;text-align:center">∅</td>
+                </tr>`;
+
+        for (const [name, b] of Object.entries(branches).sort()) {
+            html += `<tr onclick="_mediatorSelectBranch('${esc(name)}')" style="cursor:pointer;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-subtle)" onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(99,102,241,0.05)'" onmouseout="this.style.background=''">
+                <td style="padding:3px 8px 3px 0"><a href="javascript:void(0)" style="color:var(--accent);text-decoration:none">${esc(name)}</a></td>
+                <td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:center">${b.total}</td>
+                <td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:center;color:var(--success)">${b.cached}</td>
+                <td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:center;color:#d97706">${b.stale}</td>
+                <td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted)">${b.cold}</td>
+            </tr>`;
+        }
+        html += `</table></div>`;
+        detail.innerHTML = html;
+        // Adaptive graph: show all on root
+        if (_mediatorDiagData) _mediatorRenderGraph(_mediatorDiagData);
+    }
+
+    // ── Branch selection (overview) ──────────────────────────────
+
+    function _mediatorSelectBranch(branch) {
+        _mediatorSelectedPath = null;
+        _mediatorSelectedBranch = branch;
+        if (_mediatorDiagData) _mediatorRenderTree(_mediatorDiagData);
+
+        const detail = document.getElementById('mediator-detail');
+        if (!detail || !_mediatorDiagData) return;
+
+        const entries = _mediatorDiagData.entries || {};
+        const refreshing = _mediatorDiagData.refreshing || [];
+        const paths = Object.keys(entries).filter(p => p.split('.')[0] === branch).sort();
+
+        // Count stats
+        let cached = 0, stale = 0, cold = 0, refr = 0;
+        let totalAge = 0, agedCount = 0;
+        for (const p of paths) {
+            const e = entries[p];
+            if (refreshing.includes(p)) { refr++; }
+            else if (e && e.stale) { stale++; }
+            else if (e && e.cached !== false && e.age_s !== undefined) { cached++; }
+            else { cold++; }
+            if (e && e.age_s !== undefined) {
+                totalAge += e.age_s;
+                agedCount++;
+            }
+        }
+        const avgAge = agedCount > 0 ? (totalAge / agedCount) : 0;
+
+        let html = `<div style="padding:12px 16px">
+            <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px">
+                <span style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--text-primary)">${esc(branch)} branch</span>
+                <div style="display:flex;gap:6px">
+                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-ghost" onclick="_mediatorRefreshBranch('${esc(branch)}')" style="font-size:0.65rem">🔄 Refresh Branch</button>
+                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-ghost" onclick="_mediatorBustBranch('${esc(branch)}')" style="font-size:0.65rem">💥 Bust Branch</button>
+                </div>
+            </div>`;
+
+        // Stats bar
+        html += `<div style="display:flex;gap:16px;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:var(--radius-sm);background:var(--bg-secondary);font-size:0.72rem;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">
+            <span>${paths.length} nodes</span>
+            <span style="color:var(--success)">${cached} fresh</span>
+            <span style="color:#d97706">${stale} stale</span>
+            <span style="color:var(--text-muted)">${cold} cold</span>`;
+        if (refr > 0) html += `<span style="color:#6366f1">${refr} refreshing</span>`;
+        if (agedCount > 0) html += `<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">avg age: ${_mediatorFormatAge(avgAge)}</span>`;
+        html += `</div>`;
+
+        // Node list
+        html += `<table style="width:100%;font-size:0.72rem;border-collapse:collapse;font-family:var(--font-mono,'Consolas','Monaco',monospace)">
+            <tr style="color:var(--text-muted);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-subtle)">
+                <td style="padding:4px 8px 4px 0">Node</td>
+                <td style="padding:4px 8px;text-align:center">Status</td>
+                <td style="padding:4px 8px;text-align:right">Age</td>
+                <td style="padding:4px 8px;text-align:right">TTL</td>
+            </tr>`;
+
+        for (const p of paths) {
+            const e = entries[p];
+            const isRefr = refreshing.includes(p);
+            const shortName = p.split('.').slice(1).join('.') || p;
+
+            let statusIcon;
+            if (isRefr) statusIcon = '🔄';
+            else if (e && e.stale) statusIcon = '<span style="color:#d97706">⚠</span>';
+            else if (e && e.cached !== false && e.age_s !== undefined) statusIcon = '<span style="color:var(--success)">✓</span>';
+            else statusIcon = '<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">∅</span>';
+
+            const age = (e && e.age_s !== undefined) ? _mediatorFormatAge(e.age_s) : '—';
+            const ttl = e ? (e.ttl === null ? '∞' : (e.ttl === 0 ? '∞' : e.ttl + 's')) : '—';
+
+            html += `<tr onclick="_mediatorSelectNode('${esc(p)}')" style="cursor:pointer;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-subtle)" onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(99,102,241,0.05)'" onmouseout="this.style.background=''">
+                <td style="padding:3px 8px 3px 0"><a href="javascript:void(0)" style="color:var(--accent);text-decoration:none">${esc(shortName)}</a></td>
+                <td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:center">${statusIcon}</td>
+                <td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;color:var(--text-muted)">${age}</td>
+                <td style="padding:3px 8px;text-align:right;color:var(--text-muted)">${ttl}</td>
+            </tr>`;
+        }
+
+        html += `</table></div>`;
+        detail.innerHTML = html;
+        // Adaptive graph: filter to this branch
+        if (_mediatorDiagData) _mediatorRenderGraph(_mediatorDiagData);
+    }
+
+    async function _mediatorRefreshBranch(branch) {
+        try {
+            const result = await apiPost('/mediator/refresh-branch', { prefix: branch });
+            const count = Object.keys(result.refreshed || {}).length;
+            toast(`Refreshed ${count} node${count !== 1 ? 's' : ''} in ${esc(branch)} branch`);
+            await loadMediatorDiag();
+        } catch(e) {
+            toast('Branch refresh failed: ' + e.message, 'error');
+        }
+    }
+
+    async function _mediatorBustBranch(branch) {
+        try {
+            const result = await apiPost('/mediator/bust', { max_age: 0, prefix: branch });
+            toast(`Busted ${result.count || 0} entr${(result.count || 0) !== 1 ? 'ies' : 'y'} in ${esc(branch)} branch`);
+            await loadMediatorDiag();
+        } catch(e) {
+            toast('Branch bust failed: ' + e.message, 'error');
+        }
+    }
+
+    // ── Node selection & detail ───────────────────────────────────
+
+    async function _mediatorSelectNode(path) {
+        _mediatorSelectedPath = path;
+        _mediatorSelectedBranch = null;
+        if (_mediatorDiagData) _mediatorRenderTree(_mediatorDiagData);
+
+        const detail = document.getElementById('mediator-detail');
+        if (!detail) return;
+
+        detail.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:20px;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.74rem"><span class="spinner"></span>&nbsp; Loading detail…</div>';
+
+        try {
+            const data = await api('/mediator/diag/' + encodeURIComponent(path).replace(/%2E/g, '.'));
+            _mediatorRenderDetail(data);
+            // Adaptive graph: show ego graph for this node
+            if (_mediatorDiagData) _mediatorRenderGraph(_mediatorDiagData);
+        } catch(e) {
+            detail.innerHTML = `<div style="padding:20px;color:var(--danger);font-size:0.74rem">⚠ ${esc(e.message)}</div>`;
+        }
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorRenderDetail(data) {
+        const detail = document.getElementById('mediator-detail');
+        if (!detail || !data) return;
+
+        const path = data.path || '?';
+
+        // Status badge
+        let statusBadge;
+        if (data.refreshing) {
+            statusBadge = '<span style="background:rgba(99,102,241,0.12);color:#6366f1;padding:2px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-weight:500;font-size:0.72rem">🔄 REFRESHING</span>';
+        } else if (data.stale) {
+            statusBadge = '<span style="background:rgba(251,191,36,0.15);color:#d97706;padding:2px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-weight:500;font-size:0.72rem">⚠ STALE</span>';
+        } else if (data.cached) {
+            statusBadge = '<span style="background:rgba(52,211,153,0.12);color:#059669;padding:2px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-weight:500;font-size:0.72rem">● FRESH</span>';
+        } else {
+            statusBadge = '<span style="background:rgba(148,163,184,0.12);color:#64748b;padding:2px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-weight:500;font-size:0.72rem">○ COLD</span>';
+        }
+
+        // Header with title + action buttons
+        let html = `<div style="padding:12px 16px">
+            <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px">
+                <span style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.82rem;color:var(--text-primary)">${esc(path)}</span>
+                <div style="display:flex;gap:6px">
+                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-ghost" onclick="_mediatorRefreshPath('${esc(path)}')" style="font-size:0.65rem">🔄 Refresh</button>
+                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-ghost" onclick="_mediatorBustPath('${esc(path)}')" style="font-size:0.65rem">💥 Bust</button>
+                </div>
+            </div>`;
+
+        // Properties table
+        html += `<table style="width:100%;font-size:0.73rem;border-collapse:collapse;font-family:var(--font-mono,'Consolas','Monaco',monospace)">`;
+        html += _detailRow('Status', statusBadge);
+        html += _detailRow('Source', data.cached ? esc(data.source || '—') : '—');
+        html += _detailRow('Cached', data.cached ? '<span style="color:var(--success)">✓ yes</span>' : '<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">✗ no</span>');
+
+        if (data.cached) {
+            html += _detailRow('Age', data.age_s !== undefined ? `${data.age_s}s` : '—');
+
+            const ttlStr = data.ttl === null ? '∞ (no expiry)' : data.ttl === 0 ? '0 (always recompute)' : `${data.ttl}s`;
+            html += _detailRow('TTL', ttlStr);
+
+            if (data.ttl_remaining_s !== undefined) {
+                const isExpired = data.stale;
+                const remainColor = isExpired ? 'var(--danger)' : 'var(--success)';
+                const remainText = isExpired
+                    ? `-${Math.abs(data.ttl_remaining_s)}s (expired)`
+                    : `${data.ttl_remaining_s}s`;
+                html += _detailRow('Remaining', `<span style="color:${remainColor}">${remainText}</span>`);
+            }
+
+            html += _detailRow('Seq', `#${data.seq}`);
+            html += _detailRow('Last Δ', data.last_change_seq !== undefined ? `seq #${data.last_change_seq}` : '—');
+            html += _detailRow('Compute', data.elapsed_s !== undefined ? `${data.elapsed_s}s` : '—');
+        } else {
+            html += _detailRow('Last Δ', data.last_change_seq !== undefined ? `seq #${data.last_change_seq}` : '—');
+        }
+
+        html += _detailRow('Persist', data.persist ? '<span style="color:var(--success)">✓ file-backed</span>' : '<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">✗ memory only</span>');
+        html += _detailRow('Refreshing', data.refreshing ? '<span style="color:#6366f1">🔄 yes</span>' : '<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">✗</span>');
+        html += `</table>`;
+
+        // Dependencies section
+        if ((data.depends_on && data.depends_on.length > 0) || (data.dependents && data.dependents.length > 0)) {
+            html += `<div style="margin-top:12px;padding-top:10px;border-top:1px solid var(--border-subtle)">
+                <div style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-bottom:6px">── Dependencies ──</div>`;
+
+            if (data.depends_on && data.depends_on.length > 0) {
+                html += `<div style="font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-bottom:2px">Depends on:</div>`;
+                for (const d of data.depends_on) {
+                    html += `<div style="padding:1px 0 1px 12px"><a href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="_mediatorSelectNode('${esc(d)}')" style="color:var(--accent);text-decoration:none;font-size:0.72rem">${esc(d)}</a></div>`;
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (data.dependents && data.dependents.length > 0) {
+                html += `<div style="font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:2px">Depended on by:</div>`;
+                for (const d of data.dependents) {
+                    html += `<div style="padding:1px 0 1px 12px"><a href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="_mediatorSelectNode('${esc(d)}')" style="color:var(--accent);text-decoration:none;font-size:0.72rem">${esc(d)}</a></div>`;
+                }
+            }
+            html += `</div>`;
+        }
+
+        // Children
+        if (data.children && data.children.length > 0) {
+            html += `<div style="margin-top:8px">
+                <div style="font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-bottom:2px">Children:</div>`;
+            for (const c of data.children) {
+                html += `<div style="padding:1px 0 1px 12px"><a href="javascript:void(0)" onclick="_mediatorSelectNode('${esc(c)}')" style="color:var(--accent);text-decoration:none;font-size:0.72rem">${esc(c)}</a></div>`;
+            }
+            html += `</div>`;
+        }
+
+        html += `</div>`;
+        detail.innerHTML = html;
+    }
+
+    function _detailRow(label, value) {
+        return `<tr>
+            <td style="padding:3px 12px 3px 0;color:var(--text-muted);white-space:nowrap;vertical-align:top;width:90px">${label}</td>
+            <td style="padding:3px 0;color:var(--text-primary)">${value}</td>
+        </tr>`;
+    }
+
+    // ── Actions ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+    async function _mediatorRefreshPath(path) {
+        try {
+            const result = await apiPost('/mediator/refresh', { paths: [path] });
+            const count = Object.keys(result.refreshed || {}).length;
+            const errors = Object.keys(result.errors || {}).length;
+            toast(`Refreshed ${count} node${count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}${errors ? ` (${errors} error${errors !== 1 ? 's' : ''})` : ''} in ${(result.elapsed_s || 0).toFixed(2)}s`);
+            await loadMediatorDiag();
+            if (_mediatorSelectedPath === path) _mediatorSelectNode(path);
+        } catch(e) {
+            toast('Refresh failed: ' + e.message, 'error');
+        }
+    }
+
+    async function _mediatorRefreshStale() {
+        try {
+            const result = await apiPost('/mediator/refresh-stale', {});
+            const count = Object.keys(result.refreshed || {}).length;
+            toast(`Refreshed ${count} stale node${count !== 1 ? 's' : ''} in ${(result.elapsed_s || 0).toFixed(2)}s`);
+            await loadMediatorDiag();
+        } catch(e) {
+            toast('Refresh stale failed: ' + e.message, 'error');
+        }
+    }
+
+    async function _mediatorBust(maxAge) {
+        try {
+            const result = await apiPost('/mediator/bust', { max_age: maxAge });
+            toast(`Busted ${result.count || 0} entr${(result.count || 0) !== 1 ? 'ies' : 'y'} older than ${maxAge}s`);
+            await loadMediatorDiag();
+        } catch(e) {
+            toast('Bust failed: ' + e.message, 'error');
+        }
+    }
+
+    async function _mediatorBustPath(path) {
+        try {
+            const result = await apiPost('/mediator/bust', { max_age: 0, prefix: path });
+            toast(`Busted ${result.count || 0} entr${(result.count || 0) !== 1 ? 'ies' : 'y'} for ${path}`);
+            await loadMediatorDiag();
+            if (_mediatorSelectedPath === path) _mediatorSelectNode(path);
+        } catch(e) {
+            toast('Bust failed: ' + e.message, 'error');
+        }
+    }
+
+    // ── Age timer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+    function _mediatorStartAgeTimer() {
+        _mediatorStopAgeTimer();
+        _mediatorAgeTimer = setInterval(() => {
+            const rows = document.querySelectorAll('[data-mediator-path]');
+            const now = Date.now() / 1000;
+            rows.forEach(row => {
+                const computedAt = parseFloat(row.dataset.computedAt);
+                if (!computedAt) return;
+                const ageEl = row.querySelector('.mediator-age');
+                if (ageEl) {
+                    ageEl.textContent = _mediatorFormatAge(now - computedAt);
+                }
+            });
+        }, 5000);
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorStopAgeTimer() {
+        if (_mediatorAgeTimer) {
+            clearInterval(_mediatorAgeTimer);
+            _mediatorAgeTimer = null;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // ── Dependency Graph (Phase 7D) ──────────────────────────────
+
+    function _mediatorRenderGraph(data) {
+        const container = document.getElementById('mediator-graph');
+        if (!container || !data || !data.entries) { if (container) container.innerHTML = ''; return; }
+
+        const entries = data.entries;
+        const allPaths = Object.keys(entries);
+        const refreshing = data.refreshing || [];
+
+        // Build all edges: { target: path, sources: [...], downstreams: [...] }
+        const allEdges = [];
+        const sourceMap = {};
+        for (const p of allPaths) {
+            const e = entries[p];
+            if (e.depends_on && e.depends_on.length > 0) {
+                allEdges.push({ target: p, sources: e.depends_on, downstreams: e.dependents || [] });
+                sourceMap[p] = e.depends_on;
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (allEdges.length === 0) {
+            container.innerHTML = `<div style="padding:12px 0;font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-muted)">No dependency edges registered.</div>`;
+            return;
+        }
+
+        // Filter edges based on current selection
+        let filteredEdges = allEdges;
+        let filterLabel = 'all nodes';
+
+        if (_mediatorSelectedPath) {
+            // Node ego graph: show edges where this node is source, target, or downstream
+            const node = _mediatorSelectedPath;
+            filteredEdges = allEdges.filter(edge =>
+                edge.target === node ||
+                edge.sources.includes(node) ||
+                edge.downstreams.includes(node)
+            );
+            filterLabel = node;
+        } else if (_mediatorSelectedBranch) {
+            // Branch filter: show edges where any participant belongs to this branch
+            const branch = _mediatorSelectedBranch;
+            filteredEdges = allEdges.filter(edge => {
+                if (edge.target.split('.')[0] === branch) return true;
+                if (edge.sources.some(s => s.split('.')[0] === branch)) return true;
+                if (edge.downstreams.some(d => d.split('.')[0] === branch)) return true;
+                return false;
+            });
+            filterLabel = branch + ' branch';
+        }
+
+        // Header
+        let html = `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:10px">
+            <span style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.76rem;color:var(--text-secondary)">📊 Dependency Graph</span>
+            <span style="font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text-muted);background:var(--bg-secondary);padding:1px 8px;border-radius:10px">${esc(filterLabel)}</span>
+        </div>`;
+
+        if (filteredEdges.length === 0) {
+            html += `<div style="padding:12px 16px;font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-muted);border:1px solid var(--border-subtle);border-radius:var(--radius-sm);background:var(--bg-secondary)">No dependency edges for this selection.</div>`;
+            container.innerHTML = html;
+            return;
+        }
+
+        // Group filtered edges by target branch for visual separation
+        const branchEdges = {};
+        for (const edge of filteredEdges) {
+            const branch = edge.target.split('.')[0];
+            if (!branchEdges[branch]) branchEdges[branch] = [];
+            branchEdges[branch].push(edge);
+        }
+        const branches = Object.keys(branchEdges).sort();
+
+        html += `<div style="border:1px solid var(--border-subtle);border-radius:var(--radius-sm);padding:12px 16px;background:var(--bg-secondary);overflow-x:auto">`;
+
+        for (let bi = 0; bi < branches.length; bi++) {
+            const branch = branches[bi];
+            const edges = branchEdges[branch];
+
+            if (bi > 0) {
+                html += `<div style="font-size:0.68rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin:12px 0 8px;border-top:1px dashed var(--border-subtle);padding-top:8px">─── ${esc(branch)} ───</div>`;
+            }
+
+            for (const edge of edges) {
+                html += _mediatorGraphChain(edge.sources, edge.target, edge.downstreams, entries, refreshing);
+            }
+        }
+
+        html += `</div>`;
+        container.innerHTML = html;
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorGraphChain(sources, target, downstreams, entries, refreshing) {
+        // Build ASCII-style chain using monospace font
+        let html = `<div style="font-family:var(--font-mono,'Consolas','Monaco',monospace);font-size:0.68rem;line-height:1.7;margin-bottom:2px">`;
+
+        if (sources.length === 0) {
+            // Just target → downstreams
+            html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0;flex-wrap:wrap">`;
+            html += _graphNodeSpan(target, entries[target], refreshing);
+            if (downstreams.length > 0) {
+                html += `<span style="color:var(--text-muted)"> ──▶ </span>`;
+                html += downstreams.map(d => _graphNodeSpan(d, entries[d], refreshing)).join(`<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">, </span>`);
+            }
+            html += `</div>`;
+        } else if (sources.length === 1) {
+            // Single source ──▶ target ──▶ downstreams
+            html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0;flex-wrap:wrap">`;
+            html += _graphNodeSpan(sources[0], entries[sources[0]], refreshing);
+            html += `<span style="color:var(--text-muted)"> ────▶ </span>`;
+            html += _graphNodeSpan(target, entries[target], refreshing);
+            if (downstreams.length > 0) {
+                html += `<span style="color:var(--text-muted)"> ──▶ </span>`;
+                html += downstreams.map(d => _graphNodeSpan(d, entries[d], refreshing)).join(`<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">, </span>`);
+            }
+            html += `</div>`;
+        } else {
+            // Multiple sources with box-drawing connectors
+            for (let i = 0; i < sources.length; i++) {
+                const isFirst = (i === 0);
+                const isLast = (i === sources.length - 1);
+                const isMid = !isFirst && !isLast;
+
+                let connector;
+                if (sources.length === 2) {
+                    connector = isFirst ? ' ─┐' : ' ─┘';
+                } else {
+                    connector = isFirst ? ' ─┐' : (isLast ? ' ─┘' : ' ─┤');
+                }
+
+                html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0">`;
+                html += _graphNodeSpan(sources[i], entries[sources[i]], refreshing);
+                html += `<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">${connector}</span>`;
+
+                // On the middle (or last for 2) line, show ──▶ target ──▶ downstreams
+                const midIndex = Math.floor(sources.length / 2);
+                if (i === midIndex) {
+                    html += `<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">──▶ </span>`;
+                    html += _graphNodeSpan(target, entries[target], refreshing);
+                    if (downstreams.length > 0) {
+                        html += `<span style="color:var(--text-muted)"> ──▶ </span>`;
+                        html += downstreams.map(d => _graphNodeSpan(d, entries[d], refreshing)).join(`<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">, </span>`);
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    // Add vertical connector line padding
+                    if (i < midIndex) {
+                        html += `<span style="color:var(--text-muted);visibility:hidden">──▶ </span>`;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                html += `</div>`;
+            }
+        }
+
+        html += `</div>`;
+        return html;
+    }
+
+    function _graphNodeSpan(path, entry, refreshingList) {
+        const isRefreshing = refreshingList.includes(path);
+        let color;
+        if (isRefreshing) {
+            color = '#6366f1';
+        } else if (entry && entry.stale) {
+            color = '#d97706';
+        } else if (entry && entry.cached === false) {
+            color = '#94a3b8';
+        } else if (entry && entry.age_s !== undefined) {
+            color = '#34d399';
+        } else {
+            color = '#94a3b8';
+        }
+
+        const shortName = path.split('.').length > 2
+            ? path.split('.').slice(-2).join('.')
+            : path;
+
+        return `<span onclick="_mediatorSelectNode('${esc(path)}')" title="${esc(path)}" style="cursor:pointer;color:${color};font-weight:500;white-space:nowrap" onmouseover="this.style.textDecoration='underline'" onmouseout="this.style.textDecoration='none'">${esc(shortName)}</span>`;
+    }
+
+    // (dropdown filter and tag functions removed — graph is now driven by tree selection)
+
+    // ── Live Event Stream (Phase 7E) ────────────────────────────
+
+    function _mediatorRenderEventStream() {
+        const container = document.getElementById('mediator-events');
+        if (!container) return;
+
+        const pauseLabel = _mediatorEventsPaused ? 'Resume' : 'Pause';
+        const connDot = (typeof _sse !== 'undefined') && _sse && _sse.connected
+            ? '<span style="color:#34d399">●</span> Connected'
+            : '<span style="color:#94a3b8">○</span> Disconnected';
+
+        container.innerHTML = `
+            <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:8px">
+                <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
+                    <span style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.76rem;color:var(--text-secondary)">⚡ Live Events</span>
+                    <span style="font-size:0.68rem;color:var(--text-muted)">${connDot}</span>
+                    <span class="mediator-event-count" style="font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text-muted)">${_mediatorEvents.length} event${_mediatorEvents.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}</span>
+                </div>
+                <div style="display:flex;gap:6px">
+                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-ghost" onclick="_mediatorClearEvents()" style="font-size:0.65rem">Clear</button>
+                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-ghost" id="mediator-pause-btn" onclick="_mediatorTogglePause()" style="font-size:0.65rem">${pauseLabel}</button>
+                </div>
+            </div>
+            <div id="mediator-event-list" style="max-height:280px;overflow-y:auto;border:1px solid var(--border-subtle);border-radius:var(--radius-sm);background:var(--bg-secondary);font-family:var(--font-mono,'Consolas','Monaco',monospace);font-size:0.66rem"></div>`;
+
+        // Re-render existing events
+        const list = document.getElementById('mediator-event-list');
+        if (list && _mediatorEvents.length > 0) {
+            let html = '';
+            for (const evt of _mediatorEvents) {
+                html += _mediatorEventRow(evt, false);
+            }
+            list.innerHTML = html;
+        }
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorStartListening() {
+        _mediatorStopListening();
+        _mediatorEventsListener = function(e) {
+            const evt = e.detail;
+            if (!evt) return;
+
+            _mediatorEvents.unshift(evt);
+            if (_mediatorEvents.length > 100) _mediatorEvents.pop();
+
+            _mediatorAppendEvent(evt);
+
+            // Update event counter
+            const counterEl = document.querySelector('#mediator-events .mediator-event-count');
+            if (counterEl) counterEl.textContent = _mediatorEvents.length + ' event' + (_mediatorEvents.length !== 1 ? 's' : '');
+
+            // Refresh tree/summary when data changes (write or invalidated)
+            if ((evt.type === 'write' || evt.type === 'invalidated') && _mediatorDiagData) {
+                // Debounce: refresh at most every 2 seconds
+                if (!_mediatorStartListening._refreshTimer) {
+                    _mediatorStartListening._refreshTimer = setTimeout(() => {
+                        _mediatorStartListening._refreshTimer = null;
+                        loadMediatorDiag();
+                    }, 2000);
+                }
+            }
+        };
+        document.addEventListener('mediator:event', _mediatorEventsListener);
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorStopListening() {
+        if (_mediatorEventsListener) {
+            document.removeEventListener('mediator:event', _mediatorEventsListener);
+            _mediatorEventsListener = null;
+        }
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorAppendEvent(evt) {
+        const list = document.getElementById('mediator-event-list');
+        if (!list) return;
+
+        const row = document.createElement('div');
+        row.innerHTML = _mediatorEventRow(evt, true);
+        const rowEl = row.firstElementChild;
+        if (!rowEl) return;
+
+        if (list.firstChild) {
+            list.insertBefore(rowEl, list.firstChild);
+        } else {
+            list.appendChild(rowEl);
+        }
+
+        while (list.children.length > 100) {
+            list.removeChild(list.lastChild);
+        }
+
+        if (!_mediatorEventsPaused) {
+            list.scrollTop = 0;
+        }
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorEventRow(evt, animate) {
+        const ts = new Date(evt.ts);
+        const timeStr = ts.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour12: false, hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' });
+
+        const icons = {
+            write: '✏️', invalidated: '💥', cascade: '💥',
+            refresh: '🔄', bust: '💥', dispatch: '📡',
+            stale: '📡', get: '📥',
+            // Index progress events
+            'cycle:start': '🚀', 'cycle:done': '✅',
+            'node:start': '⏳', 'node:done': '✅', 'node:failed': '❌',
+            'dispatch': '📡', 'watcher:change': '👁️',
+        };
+        const icon = icons[evt.type] || '•';
+
+        const typeLabel = (evt.type || '').toUpperCase();
+        const typeColors = {
+            write: '#6366f1', invalidated: '#d97706', cascade: '#d97706',
+            refresh: '#059669', bust: '#d97706', dispatch: '#6366f1',
+            stale: '#d97706', get: 'var(--text-muted)',
+            // Index progress events
+            'cycle:start': '#818cf8', 'cycle:done': '#34d399',
+            'node:start': '#818cf8', 'node:done': '#34d399', 'node:failed': '#ef4444',
+            'watcher:change': '#f59e0b',
+        };
+        const typeColor = typeColors[evt.type] || 'var(--text-muted)';
+
+        const paths = (evt.paths || []);
+        const pathStr = paths.length > 2
+            ? paths.slice(0, 2).join(', ') + ` +${paths.length - 2}`
+            : paths.join(', ');
+
+        const firstPath = paths[0] || '';
+        const clickAttr = firstPath ? `onclick="_mediatorSelectNode('${esc(firstPath)}')"` : '';
+
+        // Build extra info based on event type
+        let extra = '';
+        if (evt.seq) extra = `seq #${evt.seq}`;
+        if (evt.elapsed_ms != null) extra += `  ${evt.elapsed_ms}ms`;
+        if (evt.entries != null) extra += `  ${evt.entries} entries`;
+        if (evt.step && evt.total) extra += `  [${evt.step}/${evt.total}]`;
+        if (evt.ok != null && evt.total) extra += `  ${evt.ok}/${evt.total} ok`;
+        if (evt.count) extra += `  ${evt.count} nodes`;
+        if (evt.error) extra += `  ⚠ ${evt.error.substring(0, 60)}`;
+        if (evt.empty === true) extra += '  (empty delta)';
+        if (evt.task_id) extra += `  ${evt.task_id}`;
+        if (evt.trigger && !evt.step) extra += `  (${evt.trigger})`;
+
+        const fadeIn = animate ? 'animation:mediatorFadeIn 0.3s ease-out' : '';
+
+        return `<div ${clickAttr} style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:3px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-subtle);${fadeIn};cursor:${firstPath ? 'pointer' : 'default'}" title="${esc(paths.join(', '))}">
+            <span style="color:var(--text-muted);min-width:56px">${timeStr}</span>
+            <span style="min-width:16px;text-align:center">${icon}</span>
+            <span style="color:${typeColor};font-weight:600;min-width:68px">${typeLabel}</span>
+            <span style="flex:1;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--text-primary)">${esc(pathStr)}</span>
+            <span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.6rem;text-align:right;min-width:80px">${esc(extra)}</span>
+        </div>`;
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorClearEvents() {
+        _mediatorEvents = [];
+        const list = document.getElementById('mediator-event-list');
+        if (list) list.innerHTML = '';
+    }
+
+    function _mediatorTogglePause() {
+        _mediatorEventsPaused = !_mediatorEventsPaused;
+        const btn = document.getElementById('mediator-pause-btn');
+        if (btn) btn.textContent = _mediatorEventsPaused ? 'Resume' : 'Pause';
+    }
+
+    // ── Live Index Status Panel ─────────────────────────────────
+    // Tracks per-node state in real time via SSE events.
+    // State: { path: { status: 'pending'|'computing'|'done'|'failed', elapsed_ms, entries, error, ts } }
+    let _statusNodes = {};
+    let _statusListener = null;
+    let _statusCycleInfo = null;  // { phase, total, startTs }
+    let _statusVisible = false;
+
+    function _mediatorShowStatus() {
+        const container = document.getElementById('debug-mediator-status');
+        if (!container) return;
+
+        // Render the accumulated state instantly — SSE events keep it updated
+        _statusRender(container);
+    }
+
+    function _statusStartListening() {
+        _statusStopListening();
+        _statusListener = function(e) {
+            const evt = e.detail;
+            if (!evt) return;
+
+            const path = (evt.paths && evt.paths[0]) || '';
+            const type = evt.type || '';
+
+            if (type === 'cycle:start') {
+                _statusCycleInfo = { phase: evt.trigger, total: evt.total, startTs: Date.now(), ok: 0, failed: 0 };
+                // Reset all nodes to pending
+                for (const p of (evt.paths || [])) {
+                    _statusNodes[p] = { status: 'pending', elapsed_ms: null, entries: null, error: null, ts: Date.now() };
+                }
+            } else if (type === 'node:start' && path) {
+                _statusNodes[path] = { status: 'computing', elapsed_ms: null, entries: null, error: null, ts: Date.now() };
+            } else if (type === 'node:done' && path) {
+                _statusNodes[path] = { status: 'done', elapsed_ms: evt.elapsed_ms, entries: evt.entries, error: null, ts: Date.now() };
+                if (_statusCycleInfo) _statusCycleInfo.ok++;
+            } else if (type === 'node:failed' && path) {
+                _statusNodes[path] = { status: 'failed', elapsed_ms: evt.elapsed_ms, entries: null, error: evt.error, ts: Date.now() };
+                if (_statusCycleInfo) _statusCycleInfo.failed++;
+            } else if (type === 'cycle:done') {
+                if (_statusCycleInfo) {
+                    _statusCycleInfo.elapsed_ms = evt.elapsed_ms;
+                    _statusCycleInfo.ok = evt.ok;
+                }
+            } else if (type === 'dispatch') {
+                // Background dispatch — mark those nodes as computing
+                for (const p of (evt.paths || [])) {
+                    if (!_statusNodes[p] || _statusNodes[p].status === 'pending') {
+                        _statusNodes[p] = { status: 'computing', elapsed_ms: null, entries: null, error: null, ts: Date.now() };
+                    }
+                }
+            } else if (type === 'write' && path) {
+                // A resolver computed — mark as done
+                if (_statusNodes[path] && _statusNodes[path].status === 'computing') {
+                    _statusNodes[path].status = 'done';
+                    _statusNodes[path].ts = Date.now();
+                }
+            }
+
+            // Re-render
+            const container = document.getElementById('debug-mediator-status');
+            if (container && _statusVisible) _statusRender(container);
+        };
+        document.addEventListener('mediator:event', _statusListener);
+    }
+
+    function _statusStopListening() {
+        if (_statusListener) {
+            document.removeEventListener('mediator:event', _statusListener);
+            _statusListener = null;
+        }
+    }
+
+    function _statusRender(container) {
+        const allPaths = Object.keys(_statusNodes).sort();
+        const counts = { pending: 0, computing: 0, done: 0, failed: 0 };
+        for (const n of Object.values(_statusNodes)) counts[n.status]++;
+        const total = allPaths.length;
+        const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((counts.done / total) * 100) : 0;
+
+        let html = '<div style="padding:12px 16px;font-size:0.73rem;font-family:var(--font-mono,Consolas,Monaco,monospace)">';
+
+        // Title + cycle info
+        html += '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:10px">';
+        html += '<span style="font-weight:700;font-size:0.85rem;color:var(--text-primary)">📈 Live Index Status</span>';
+        if (_statusCycleInfo && _statusCycleInfo.elapsed_ms) {
+            html += '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.68rem">Last cycle: ' + _statusCycleInfo.elapsed_ms + 'ms</span>';
+        } else if (_statusCycleInfo && !_statusCycleInfo.elapsed_ms) {
+            html += '<span style="color:#818cf8;font-size:0.68rem">⏳ Computing...</span>';
+        }
+        html += '</div>';
+
+        // Progress bar
+        html += '<div style="height:6px;background:var(--bg-tertiary,#2a2a3e);border-radius:3px;margin-bottom:8px;overflow:hidden">';
+        html += '<div style="height:100%;width:' + pct + '%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#818cf8,#34d399);border-radius:3px;transition:width 0.3s"></div>';
+        html += '</div>';
+
+        // Summary counts
+        html += '<div style="display:flex;gap:14px;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">';
+        html += '<span style="color:#34d399">✅ ' + counts.done + ' done</span>';
+        html += '<span style="color:#818cf8">⏳ ' + counts.computing + ' computing</span>';
+        html += '<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">⬜ ' + counts.pending + ' pending</span>';
+        if (counts.failed > 0) html += '<span style="color:#ef4444">❌ ' + counts.failed + ' failed</span>';
+        html += '<span style="color:var(--text-muted)">' + pct + '%</span>';
+        html += '</div>';
+
+        // Per-node rows grouped by domain
+        const domains = {};
+        for (const p of allPaths) {
+            const d = p.split('.')[0];
+            if (!domains[d]) domains[d] = [];
+            domains[d].push(p);
+        }
+
+        for (const [domain, paths] of Object.entries(domains).sort()) {
+            const domainDone = paths.filter(p => _statusNodes[p].status === 'done').length;
+            const domainColor = domainDone === paths.length ? '#34d399' : domainDone === 0 ? 'var(--text-muted)' : '#f59e0b';
+            html += '<div style="margin-bottom:8px">';
+            html += '<div style="font-weight:600;color:' + domainColor + ';margin-bottom:3px;font-size:0.72rem">' + domain + ' (' + domainDone + '/' + paths.length + ')</div>';
+
+            for (const p of paths) {
+                const n = _statusNodes[p];
+                const name = p.split('.').slice(1).join('.');
+                let icon, color, detail;
+
+                switch (n.status) {
+                    case 'computing':
+                        icon = '⏳'; color = '#818cf8'; detail = 'computing...';
+                        break;
+                    case 'done':
+                        icon = '✅'; color = '#34d399';
+                        detail = '';
+                        if (n.elapsed_ms != null) detail += n.elapsed_ms + 'ms';
+                        if (n.entries != null) detail += (detail ? ' · ' : '') + n.entries + ' entries';
+                        if (!detail) detail = 'cached';
+                        break;
+                    case 'failed':
+                        icon = '❌'; color = '#ef4444';
+                        detail = n.error ? n.error.substring(0, 80) : 'error';
+                        if (n.elapsed_ms != null) detail = n.elapsed_ms + 'ms · ' + detail;
+                        break;
+                    default:
+                        icon = '⬜'; color = 'var(--text-muted)'; detail = 'pending';
+                }
+
+                html += '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:2px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-subtle)">';
+                html += '<span style="min-width:16px">' + icon + '</span>';
+                html += '<span style="flex:1;color:' + color + '">' + name + '</span>';
+                html += '<span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:0.64rem;text-align:right">' + detail + '</span>';
+                html += '</div>';
+            }
+            html += '</div>';
+        }
+
+        html += '</div>';
+        container.innerHTML = html;
+    }
+
+    // Start capturing events immediately at page load.
+    // Data accumulates silently; renders only when views are active.
+    _statusStartListening();
+    _mediatorStartListening();
+</script>

+"""Tests for QueryMediator Phase 0 — tree registry and core mediator."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import math
+import threading
+import time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from src.core.services.mediator.core import QueryMediator
+from src.core.services.mediator.tree import DataTree, TreeRegistration
+
+
+# ── Fixtures ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def tree() -> DataTree:
+    return DataTree()
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def mediator(tree: DataTree) -> QueryMediator:
+    return QueryMediator(tree, Path("/tmp/test-project"))
+
+
+# ── DataTree tests ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+
+class TestTreeRegister:
+    """Test node registration and path resolution."""
+
+    def test_register_simple(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """Single path registers and resolves."""
+        node = tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="posture.toolchain",
+            resolver=lambda: "data",
+            ttl=300,
+        ))
+        assert node.path == "posture.toolchain"
+        assert node.ttl == 300
+        assert node.is_registered
+
+    def test_resolve(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """Registered path resolves correctly."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.b.c", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        node = tree.resolve("a.b.c")
+        assert node is not None
+        assert node.path == "a.b.c"
+
+    def test_resolve_missing(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """Non-existent path returns None."""
+        assert tree.resolve("nonexistent") is None
+
+    def test_duplicate_raises(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """Registering same path twice raises ValueError."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.b", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="already registered"):
+            tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.b", resolver=lambda: 2))
+
+    def test_empty_path_raises(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """Empty path raises ValueError."""
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="cannot be empty"):
+            tree.register(TreeRegistration(path=""))
+
+
+class TestTreeIntermediate:
+    """Test auto-creation of branch nodes."""
+
+    def test_intermediate_branches(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """Registering a.b.c auto-creates a and a.b as branches."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.b.c", resolver=lambda: 1))
+
+        branch_a = tree.resolve("a")
+        assert branch_a is not None
+        assert not branch_a.is_registered
+        assert branch_a.is_branch
+
+        branch_ab = tree.resolve("a.b")
+        assert branch_ab is not None
+        assert not branch_ab.is_registered
+        assert branch_ab.is_branch
+
+    def test_promote_branch(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """Registering at an auto-created branch path promotes it."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.b.c", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        # a.b exists as branch; now register it
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="a.b", resolver=lambda: 2, ttl=60,
+        ))
+        node = tree.resolve("a.b")
+        assert node is not None
+        assert node.is_registered
+        assert node.ttl == 60
+        # Still has child
+        assert node.is_branch
+
+    def test_children(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """children() returns direct children."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.x", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.y", resolver=lambda: 2))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.y.z", resolver=lambda: 3))
+
+        children = tree.children("a")
+        paths = [c.path for c in children]
+        assert sorted(paths) == ["a.x", "a.y"]
+
+    def test_top_level_children(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """children('') returns top-level nodes."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="alpha.x", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="beta.y", resolver=lambda: 2))
+
+        children = tree.children("")
+        paths = [c.path for c in children]
+        assert sorted(paths) == ["alpha", "beta"]
+

+class TestTreeDependencies:
+    """Test dependency tracking and cascade resolution."""
+
+    def test_depends_on(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """depends_on is stored correctly."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="a", resolver=lambda: 1,
+        ))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="b", resolver=lambda: 2,
+            depends_on=["a"],
+        ))
+
+        node_b = tree.resolve("b")
+        assert node_b is not None
+        assert node_b.depends_on == ["a"]
+
+    def test_reverse_dependents(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """Reverse dependencies are auto-computed."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="b", resolver=lambda: 2,
+            depends_on=["a"],
+        ))
+
+        node_a = tree.resolve("a")
+        assert node_a is not None
+        assert "b" in node_a.dependents
+
+    def test_transitive_dependents(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """dependents() walks transitive dependencies."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="b", resolver=lambda: 2, depends_on=["a"],
+        ))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="c", resolver=lambda: 3, depends_on=["b"],
+        ))
+
+        deps = tree.dependents("a")
+        assert "b" in deps
+        assert "c" in deps
+
+    def test_glob_dependents(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """Glob patterns in depends_on are resolved."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="detect.tools.go", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="detect.tools.docker", resolver=lambda: 2))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="posture.toolchain", resolver=lambda: 3,
+            depends_on=["detect.tools.*"],
+        ))
+
+        # detect.tools.go should have posture.toolchain as dependent
+        node_go = tree.resolve("detect.tools.go")
+        assert node_go is not None
+        assert "posture.toolchain" in node_go.dependents
+
+        # Same for docker
+        node_docker = tree.resolve("detect.tools.docker")
+        assert node_docker is not None
+        assert "posture.toolchain" in node_docker.dependents
+
+    def test_depth_limited_dependents(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """depth=0 returns only direct dependents."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="b", resolver=lambda: 2, depends_on=["a"],
+        ))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="c", resolver=lambda: 3, depends_on=["b"],
+        ))
+
+        deps = tree.dependents("a", depth=0)
+        assert deps == ["b"]  # c not included
+
+
+class TestTreeDiagnostics:
+    """Test tree introspection methods."""
+
+    def test_all_paths(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """all_paths returns only registered paths, sorted."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="b.x", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.y", resolver=lambda: 2))
+
+        paths = tree.all_paths()
+        # Should not include auto-created branches "a" and "b"
+        assert paths == ["a.y", "b.x"]
+
+    def test_stats(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """stats returns node counts."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.b", resolver=lambda: 1, persist=True))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.c", resolver=lambda: 2))
+
+        stats = tree.stats()
+        assert stats["registered"] == 2
+        assert stats["persistent"] == 1
+        assert stats["total_nodes"] >= 3  # a (branch) + a.b + a.c
+
+    def test_subtree(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """subtree returns nested dict structure."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.b", resolver=lambda: 1, ttl=60))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="a.c", resolver=lambda: 2))
+
+        st = tree.subtree("a")
+        assert st["path"] == "a"
+        assert "b" in st.get("children", {})
+        assert st["children"]["b"]["ttl"] == 60
+
+    def test_match_glob(self, tree: DataTree) -> None:
+        """match() returns nodes matching glob pattern."""
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="detect.tools.go", resolver=lambda: 1))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="detect.tools.docker", resolver=lambda: 2))
+        tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="detect.os", resolver=lambda: 3))
+
+        matches = tree.match("detect.tools.*")
+        paths = sorted(n.path for n in matches)
+        assert paths == ["detect.tools.docker", "detect.tools.go"]
+

+class TestMediatorGet:
+    """Test get() resolution."""
+
+    def test_get_miss_no_resolver(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """get() on path without resolver and no cache raises."""
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="empty"))
+        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="No resolver"):
+            mediator.get("empty")
+
+    def test_get_not_registered(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """get() on non-existent path raises KeyError."""
+        with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="not found"):
+            mediator.get("nonexistent")
+
+    def test_get_computed(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """get() calls resolver and returns data."""
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="test.value", resolver=lambda: 42, ttl=60,
+        ))
+        result = mediator.get("test.value")
+        assert result["data"] == 42
+        assert result["meta"]["source"] == "computed"
+
+    def test_get_cached(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """Second get() returns cached value."""
+        call_count = 0
+
+        def resolver():
+            nonlocal call_count
+            call_count += 1
+            return call_count
+
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="test.counter", resolver=resolver, ttl=60,
+        ))
+
+        r1 = mediator.get("test.counter")
+        r2 = mediator.get("test.counter")
+
+        assert r1["data"] == 1
+        assert r2["data"] == 1  # same value, not recomputed
+        assert r2["meta"]["source"] == "cache"
+        assert call_count == 1
+
+    def test_get_force_recomputes(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """force=True bypasses cache."""
+        call_count = 0
+
+        def resolver():
+            nonlocal call_count
+            call_count += 1
+            return call_count
+
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="test.force", resolver=resolver, ttl=60,
+        ))
+
+        mediator.get("test.force")
+        r2 = mediator.get("test.force", force=True)
+
+        assert r2["data"] == 2
+        assert r2["meta"]["source"] == "computed"
+        assert call_count == 2
+
+    def test_get_max_age(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """max_age triggers recompute when cache is older."""
+        call_count = 0
+
+        def resolver():
+            nonlocal call_count
+            call_count += 1
+            return call_count
+
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="test.age", resolver=resolver, ttl=300,
+        ))
+
+        mediator.get("test.age")
+        # Manually age the cache entry
+        entry = mediator._cache.get("test.age")
+        assert entry is not None
+        entry.computed_at = time.time() - 10  # 10 seconds old
+
+        r2 = mediator.get("test.age", max_age=5)
+        assert r2["data"] == 2  # recomputed because age > max_age
+        assert call_count == 2
+
+    def test_get_explain(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """explain=True includes resolution explanation."""
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="test.explain", resolver=lambda: "ok", ttl=60,
+        ))
+        result = mediator.get("test.explain", explain=True)
+        assert "explain" in result["meta"]
+
+    def test_get_infinite_ttl(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """Node with infinite TTL never expires."""
+        call_count = 0
+
+        def resolver():
+            nonlocal call_count
+            call_count += 1
+            return call_count
+
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="test.forever", resolver=resolver, ttl=math.inf,
+        ))
+
+        mediator.get("test.forever")
+        entry = mediator._cache.get("test.forever")
+        assert entry is not None
+        entry.computed_at = time.time() - 999999  # very old
+
+        r2 = mediator.get("test.forever")
+        assert r2["data"] == 1  # not recomputed
+        assert call_count == 1
+
+
+class TestMediatorPut:
+    """Test put() write/invalidate/cascade."""
+
+    def test_put_write(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """put(data=...) stores value in cache."""
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(path="test.put"))
+        mediator.put("test.put", data="hello")
+
+        result = mediator.get("test.put")
+        assert result["data"] == "hello"
+
+    def test_put_invalidate(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """put(data=None) removes from cache."""
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="test.inv", resolver=lambda: 1, ttl=60,
+        ))
+        mediator.get("test.inv")  # populate cache
+        result = mediator.put("test.inv")
+        assert "test.inv" in result["invalidated"]
+
+        # Next get recomputes
+        r2 = mediator.get("test.inv")
+        assert r2["meta"]["source"] == "computed"
+
+    def test_put_cascade(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """put(cascade=True) invalidates dependents."""
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="base", resolver=lambda: 1, ttl=60,
+        ))
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="derived", resolver=lambda: 2, ttl=60,
+            depends_on=["base"],
+        ))
+
+        # Populate both caches
+        mediator.get("base")
+        mediator.get("derived")
+
+        # Invalidate base with cascade
+        result = mediator.put("base", cascade=True)
+        assert "derived" in result["invalidated"]
+
+    def test_put_no_cascade(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """put(cascade=False) only invalidates the target."""
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="base2", resolver=lambda: 1, ttl=60,
+        ))
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="derived2", resolver=lambda: 2, ttl=60,
+            depends_on=["base2"],
+        ))
+
+        mediator.get("base2")
+        mediator.get("derived2")
+
+        result = mediator.put("base2", cascade=False)
+        assert "derived2" not in result["invalidated"]
+
+
+class TestMediatorDiag:
+    """Test diag() diagnostics."""
+
+    def test_diag_summary(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """diag() returns tree and cache summary."""
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="a", resolver=lambda: 1, ttl=60,
+        ))
+        mediator.get("a")  # populate cache
+
+        info = mediator.diag()
+        assert "tree" in info
+        assert "cached" in info
+        assert info["cached"] == 1
+
+    def test_diag_node_detail(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """diag(path) returns detail for one node."""
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="b", resolver=lambda: 2, ttl=60, persist=True,
+        ))
+        mediator.get("b")
+
+        info = mediator.diag("b")
+        assert info["path"] == "b"
+        assert info["cached"] is True
+        assert info["ttl"] == 60
+        assert info["persist"] is True
+        assert "age_s" in info
+
+    def test_diag_missing_path(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """diag() for non-existent path returns error."""
+        info = mediator.diag("nope")
+        assert "error" in info
+

+class TestMediatorConcurrency:
+    """Test thread safety of compute locks."""
+
+    def test_concurrent_get_dedup(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        """Multiple threads requesting same path only compute once."""
+        call_count = 0
+        lock = threading.Lock()
+
+        def slow_resolver():
+            nonlocal call_count
+            with lock:
+                call_count += 1
+            time.sleep(0.1)
+            return "result"
+
+        mediator.tree.register(TreeRegistration(
+            path="slow", resolver=slow_resolver, ttl=60,
+        ))
+
+        results: list[dict] = []
+        errors: list[Exception] = []
+
+        def worker():
+            try:
+                r = mediator.get("slow")
+                results.append(r)
+            except Exception as e:
+                errors.append(e)
+
+        threads = [threading.Thread(target=worker) for _ in range(5)]
+        for t in threads:
+            t.start()
+        for t in threads:
+            t.join(timeout=5)
+
+        assert not errors
+        assert len(results) == 5
+        assert call_count == 1  # only one computation
+        for r in results:
+            assert r["data"] == "result"

+            node = mediator.tree.resolve(f"devops.{name}")
+            assert node.depends_on == [f"detect.{name}"], (
+                f"devops.{name} deps={node.depends_on}"

+# ── Cascade chain tests ──────────────────────────────────────────
+
+
+class TestCascadeChains:
+    """Test the transitive cascade chains."""
+
+    def test_git_cascades_to_github_docker_ci(
+        self, mediator: QueryMediator
+    ) -> None:
+        """devops.git dependents should include github, docker, ci."""
+        deps = mediator.tree.dependents("devops.git")
+        assert "devops.github" in deps
+        assert "devops.docker" in deps
+        assert "devops.ci" in deps
+
+    def test_docker_cascades_to_k8s_ci(
+        self, mediator: QueryMediator
+    ) -> None:
+        """devops.docker dependents should include k8s and ci."""
+        deps = mediator.tree.dependents("devops.docker")
+        assert "devops.k8s" in deps
+        assert "devops.ci" in deps
+
+    def test_github_cascades_to_ci(self, mediator: QueryMediator) -> None:
+        deps = mediator.tree.dependents("devops.github")
+        assert "devops.ci" in deps
+
+    def test_full_chain_from_detect_git(
+        self, mediator: QueryMediator
+    ) -> None:
+        """detect.git should transitively cascade to all git-dependent cards."""
+        deps = mediator.tree.dependents("detect.git")
+        # Direct: devops.git
+        assert "devops.git" in deps
+        # Transitive via devops.git:
+        assert "devops.docker" in deps
+        assert "devops.github" in deps
+        assert "devops.ci" in deps
+        # Transitive via devops.docker:
+        assert "devops.k8s" in deps
+        # Aggregate:
+        assert "devops.status" in deps
+
+    def test_cascade_invalidation_works(
+        self, mediator: QueryMediator
+    ) -> None:
+        """Invalidating detect.git should cascade through the whole chain."""
+        m = mediator
+        # Populate caches — use cascade=False to avoid clearing
+        # dependent entries while seeding
+        m.put("detect.git", data={"v": 0}, cascade=False)
+        m.put("devops.git", data={"v": 1}, cascade=False)
+        m.put("devops.docker", data={"v": 2}, cascade=False)
+        m.put("devops.github", data={"v": 3}, cascade=False)
+        m.put("devops.ci", data={"v": 4}, cascade=False)
+        m.put("devops.k8s", data={"v": 5}, cascade=False)
+        m.put("devops.status", data={"v": 6}, cascade=False)
+
+        # Invalidate detect.git with cascade
+        result = m.put("detect.git", cascade=True)
+        inv = result["invalidated"]
+
+        assert "detect.git" in inv
+        assert "devops.git" in inv
+        assert "devops.docker" in inv
+        assert "devops.github" in inv
+        assert "devops.ci" in inv
+        assert "devops.k8s" in inv
+        assert "devops.status" in inv
+
+
+# ── Aggregate node tests ──────────────────────────────────────────
+
+
+class TestAggregateNode:
+    """Test devops.status aggregate node."""
+
+    def test_status_depends_on_all_cards(
+        self, mediator: QueryMediator
+    ) -> None:
+        """devops.status should depend on all 13 card nodes via glob."""
+        node = mediator.tree.resolve("devops.status")
+        # The actual depends_on is ["devops.*"] (glob)
+        assert "devops.*" in node.depends_on
+
+    def test_any_card_invalidation_cascades_to_status(
+        self, mediator: QueryMediator
+    ) -> None:
+        """Invalidating any devops card should cascade to devops.status."""
+        m = mediator
+        for card_name in ["docker", "k8s", "git", "github", "ci", "terraform",
+                          "env", "security", "packages", "quality", "testing",
+                          "docs", "dns"]:
+            # Populate status cache (no cascade to avoid clearing it)
+            m.put("devops.status", data={"v": "status"}, cascade=False)
+            # Populate the card itself so invalidation triggers
+            m.put(f"devops.{card_name}", data={"v": card_name}, cascade=False)
+            # Invalidate the card
+            result = m.put(f"devops.{card_name}", cascade=True)
+            assert "devops.status" in result["invalidated"], (
+                f"devops.{card_name} didn't cascade to devops.status"
+            )
+
+

+        assert "github" in keys
+        assert "ci" in keys
+        assert "project-status" in keys

+        assert keys == ["whatever"]
